【前置支撑文件（直接复制）】：请先将下方完整的 face_helpers.py 复制到 questions/face_recognizer/face_helpers.py，供 D3 与座舱迎宾模块调用。

查看代码Python
"""
人脸识别辅助函数模块
功能: 封装了人脸特征提取、数据库加载和特征比对等核心功能，供主程序调用
"""
import glob
import os
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
import dlib
import numpy as np

def get_face_feature(predictor, model, image, faces=None, detector=None):
    """
    获取单张图像中的人脸及其128维特征向量
    :param predictor: dlib的人脸关键点预测器
    :param model: dlib的人脸识别模型
    :param image: 输入的图像 (OpenCV格式)
    :param faces: (可选) 已检测到的人脸列表
    :param detector: (可选) dlib的人脸检测器，如果未提供faces，则会使用此检测器
    :return: faces列表 和 features列表
    """
    # 1. 检查输入参数的有效性
    if faces is None and detector is None:
        raise ValueError("必须提供 faces 或 detector 参数之一")

    # 2. 将图像从BGR转换为RGB，因为dlib模型使用RGB格式
    rgb_image = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)

    # 3. 如果未直接传入人脸框，则使用检测器进行检测
    if faces is None:
        faces = detector(rgb_image, 0)

    features = []
    # 4. 遍历所有检测到的人脸
    for face in faces:
        # 5. 对每个人脸，使用预测器获取关键点
        shape = predictor(rgb_image, face)
        # 6. 使用识别模型，根据关键点计算128维特征向量
        face_desc = np.array(model.compute_face_descriptor(rgb_image, shape))
        features.append(face_desc)

    return faces, features

def load_face_feature(csv_path):
    """
    从指定路径加载所有已注册的人脸特征
    :param csv_path: 存放特征文件的根目录
    :return: all_load_feature列表 和 all_person_name列表
    """
    all_load_feature = []
    all_person_name = []

    feature_dir = Path(csv_path)
    # 1. 检查特征库路径是否存在
    if not feature_dir.exists():
        return [], []

    # 2. 遍历每个以用户名命名的子文件夹
    for person_dir in feature_dir.iterdir():
        if person_dir.is_dir():
            person_name = person_dir.name
            # 3. 遍历该用户文件夹下所有的.csv特征文件
            for feature_file in person_dir.glob('*.csv'):
                # 4. 读取csv文件中的特征数据
                array = np.genfromtxt(str(feature_file), delimiter=',')
                all_load_feature.append(array)
                all_person_name.append(person_name)

    return all_load_feature, all_person_name

def face_recognition(face1, face2):
    """
    比较两个128维特征向量的欧氏距离，判断是否为同一个人
    :param face1: 特征向量1
    :param face2: 特征向量2
    :return: True表示匹配，False表示不匹配
    """
    # 1. 使用numpy计算两个向量的L2范数（欧氏距离）
    distance = np.linalg.norm(face1 - face2)
    # 2. 设置一个阈值，小于该阈值则认为匹配成功
    threshold = 0.4
    if distance < threshold:
        return True
    else:
        return False

def face_compare(predictor, detector, model, face_image, all_load_feature, all_person_name, faces=None):
    """
    人脸匹配主函数，将实时画面中的人脸与数据库进行比对
    :param predictor: dlib关键点预测器
    :param detector: dlib人脸检测器
    :param model: dlib人脸识别模型
    :param face_image: 实时视频帧
    :param all_load_feature: 数据库中的所有特征
    :param all_person_name: 数据库中特征对应的名字
    :return: result_names列表（识别出的名字） 和 result_faces列表（对应的脸部框）
    """
    # 1. 从当前视频帧中获取人脸位置和128维特征
    result_faces, live_features = get_face_feature(predictor, model, face_image, faces, detector)

    result_names = []

    # 2. 遍历当前帧中检测到的每一个人脸
    for feature in live_features:
        match_name = "Unknown"
        min_dist = float('inf')  # 用于寻找最相似的人脸

        # 3. 将当前人脸与数据库中的所有特征进行比较
        for i, db_feature in enumerate(all_load_feature):
            dist = np.linalg.norm(feature - db_feature)
            # 4. 寻找距离最小的那个特征
            if dist < min_dist:
                min_dist = dist
                # 5. 如果最小距离依然满足阈值条件，则更新识别结果
                if min_dist < 0.4:
                    match_name = all_person_name[i]
                else:
                    match_name = "Unknown"

        result_names.append(match_name)

    return result_names, result_faces
D3｜questions/face_recognizer/face_recognizer.py

用下方完整代码稿替换原类和主入口。类中的模型与特征库加载沿教师答案；主入口仅把摄像头循环换成固定照片一次处理，并保存 output/face_result.jpg。不能按照片文件名填姓名，姓名来自 face_compare()。

【综合任务 + 参数对照与个人标识】：请用下方无摄像头固定照片版覆盖根目录 questions/face_recognizer/face_recognizer.py（D3），并补全 3 处 ____ 空位（load_face_feature、face_compare、detect_core），运行生成带身份姓名的 output/face_result.jpg。参数对照要求：先用默认阈值 0.4 验证已知与未知照片，再在 face_helpers.py 的 face_compare() 中临时调整为 0.55 观察匹配放宽/误识变化后恢复 0.4；在输出图片保留 cv2.putText 绘制个人学号（或姓名拼音）并在控制台打印姓名与学号！

查看代码Python
"""
任务D3：人脸匹配与识别
功能：调用摄像头，实时检测并识别已注册的人脸
"""
import cv2
import dlib
from face_helpers import *  # 引入我们刚刚创建的帮助文件

class FaceRecognizer:
    def __init__(self):
        # ---- 模型和路径配置 ----
        predictor_path = "./data/shape_predictor_5_face_landmarks.dat"
        face_model_path = "./data/dlib_face_recognition_resnet_model_v1.dat"
        self.csv_path = "./output/features"
        # ------------------------

        # 1. 初始化dlib的人脸检测器、关键点预测器和人脸识别模型
        self.detector = dlib.get_frontal_face_detector()
        self.predictor = dlib.shape_predictor(predictor_path)
        self.model = dlib.face_recognition_model_v1(face_model_path)

        # 2. 在程序启动时，加载所有已注册的人脸特征到内存中
        self.all_db_features, self.all_db_names = ____(self.csv_path)
        if not self.all_db_features:
            print("[警告] 特征库为空，请先运行 face_register.py 进行人脸注册。")
        else:
            print(f"[信息] 特征库加载成功，包含身份: {list(set(self.all_db_names))}")

    def detect_core(self, frame, faces=None):
        """
        核心识别函数，负责处理单帧图像的检测与识别
        :param frame: 从摄像头读取的视频帧
        :return: 绘制了识别结果的视频帧
        """
        # 1. 如果特征库为空，则直接在画面上提示并返回，不进行后续处理
        if not self.all_db_features:
            cv2.putText(frame, "No Face Database!", (20, 50), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 2)
            return frame, [], []

        # 2. 调用辅助函数，完成从检测到比对的全过程
        names, faces = ____(self.predictor, self.detector, self.model,
                                    frame, self.all_db_features, self.all_db_names,
                                    faces)

        return frame, faces, names

def main():
    frame = cv2.imread("data/test_face.jpg")
    if frame is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError("data/test_face.jpg")
    recognizer = FaceRecognizer()
    shown, faces, names = recognizer.____(frame)
    for face, name in zip(faces, names):
        color = (0, 255, 0) if name != "Unknown" else (0, 0, 255)
        cv2.rectangle(shown, (face.left(), face.top()),
                      (face.right(), face.bottom()), color, 2)
        cv2.putText(shown, name, (face.left(), face.top() - 10),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.8, color, 2)
    from pathlib import Path
    Path("output").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite("output/face_result.jpg", shown)
    print(names)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

空位提示：

  • 空 1：初始化时使用已给的 load_face_feature 读取 CSV 身份库。
  • 空 2：逐帧识别调用已给的 face_compare。
  • 空 3：主入口调用当前对象 recognizer 的 detect_core。

"""questions/ui/facewidget.py：人脸检测与识别独立 PyQt 界面组件（改编自 answers/ui/facewidget.py）"""

from contextlib import contextmanager
import os
import sys

import cv2
from PyQt5.QtCore import QTimer
from PyQt5.QtGui import QImage, QPixmap
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import (
    QApplication,
    QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QPushButton,
    QTextEdit,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QWidget,
)

_MODULE_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))


def module_dir(name):
    """返回 questions/ 下指定模块目录的绝对路径，并自动加入 sys.path。"""
    path = os.path.join(_MODULE_ROOT, name)
    if path not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, path)
    return path


@contextmanager
def working_directory(path):
    """临时切换工作目录，退出后自动还原。"""
    current = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(path)
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        os.chdir(current)


module_dir("face_recognizer")
from face_detector import *
from face_recognizer import *

DEFAULT_FACE_SOURCE = os.path.join(module_dir("face_recognizer"), "data", "test_face.jpg")

class FaceWidget(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, device_num=DEFAULT_FACE_SOURCE):
        super().__init__()
        self.device_num = device_num
        self.cap = None
        self.static_frame = None
        self.face = None
        self.classifier = None
        self.camera_flag = False
        self.detect_flag = False
        self.recognize_flag = False
        self.initUI()

    def initUI(self):
        self.setWindowTitle("生物识别系统 - 学生：姓名/学号")
        layout = QVBoxLayout()

        self.face_label = QLabel("学生：姓名/学号｜点击“打开画面”加载固定照片，依次测试人脸检测与识别", self)
        layout.addWidget(self.face_label)

        layout_button = QHBoxLayout()
        self.open_button = QPushButton("打开画面", self)
        self.open_button.clicked.connect(self.open_camera)
        self.detect_button = QPushButton("开启人脸检测", self)
        self.detect_button.clicked.connect(self.start_detect)
        self.recognize_button = QPushButton("开启人脸识别", self)
        self.recognize_button.clicked.connect(self.start_recognize)
        self.close_button = QPushButton("关闭画面", self)
        self.close_button.clicked.connect(self.close_camera)
        layout_button.addWidget(self.open_button)
        layout_button.addWidget(self.detect_button)
        layout_button.addWidget(self.recognize_button)
        layout_button.addWidget(self.close_button)
        layout.addLayout(layout_button)

        layout_core = QHBoxLayout()
        self.image_label = QLabel(self)
        self.info_text = QTextEdit(self)
        self.info_text.setPlaceholderText("生物识别结果（学生：姓名/学号）：")
        self.info_text.setMinimumWidth(260)
        self.info_text.setReadOnly(True)
        layout_core.addWidget(self.image_label)
        layout_core.addWidget(self.info_text)
        layout.addLayout(layout_core)
        layout.addStretch(1)

        self.camera_timer = QTimer()
        self.camera_timer.timeout.connect(self.update_frame)
        self.camera_timer.start(250)
        self.setLayout(layout)
        self.close_button.setEnabled(False)

    def _ensure_models_loaded(self):
        if self.face is None or self.classifier is None:
            with working_directory(module_dir("face_recognizer")):
                self.face = FaceDetector(Method.HOG_DLIB)
                self.classifier = FaceRecognizer()

    def open_camera(self):
        self.camera_flag = True
        self.open_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.close_button.setEnabled(True)

    def start_detect(self):
        if self.detect_button.text() == "开启人脸检测":
            self.detect_flag = True
            self.detect_button.setText("人脸检测已开启")
        else:
            self.detect_flag = False
            self.detect_button.setText("开启人脸检测")

    def start_recognize(self):
        if self.recognize_button.text() == "开启人脸识别":
            self.recognize_flag = True
            self.recognize_button.setText("人脸识别已开启")
        else:
            self.recognize_flag = False
            self.recognize_button.setText("开启人脸识别")

    def close_camera(self):
        self.camera_flag = False
        if self.cap is not None:
            self.cap.release()
            self.cap = None
        self.static_frame = None
        self.open_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.close_button.setEnabled(False)

    def update_frame(self):
        if not self.camera_flag:
            return
        self._ensure_models_loaded()

        is_static_image = isinstance(self.device_num, str) and self.device_num.lower().endswith(
            (".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".bmp")
        )
        if is_static_image:
            if self.static_frame is None:
                self.static_frame = cv2.imread(self.device_num)
            if self.static_frame is None:
                self.append_text_and_scroll(f"读取图片失败：{self.device_num}")
                self.camera_flag = False
                return
            frame = self.static_frame.copy()
        else:
            if not self.cap or not self.cap.isOpened():
                self.cap = cv2.VideoCapture(self.device_num)
                if not self.cap.isOpened():
                    self.append_text_and_scroll(f"无法打开输入源：{self.device_num}")
                    self.camera_flag = False
                    return
            ret, frame = self.cap.read()
            if not ret:
                self.cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, 0)
                ret, frame = self.cap.read()
            if not ret or frame is None:
                return

        if self.detect_flag:
            if not self.recognize_flag:
                frame, faces, _ = self.face.detect_core(frame)
                for face in faces:
                    cv2.rectangle(frame, (face.left(), face.top()), (face.right(), face.bottom()), (0, 255, 0), 2)
                self.append_text_and_scroll(f"[学生：姓名/学号] 仅开启人脸检测：检测到 {len(faces)} 个人脸")
            else:
                _, faces, _ = self.face.detect_core(frame)
                frame, faces, names = self.classifier.detect_core(frame, faces)
                for i, name in enumerate(names):
                    face = faces[i]
                    color = (0, 255, 0) if name != "Unknown" else (0, 0, 255)
                    cv2.rectangle(frame, (face.left(), face.top()), (face.right(), face.bottom()), color, 2)
                    cv2.putText(frame, f"{name} | ID:<xuehao>", (face.left(), max(20, face.top() - 10)),
                                cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, color, 2)
                if faces:
                    known_names = "、".join([n for n in names if n != "Unknown"])
                    self.append_text_and_scroll(
                        "启用人脸识别：有人脸{}{}".format(
                            ", 已知身份: " + known_names if known_names else "",
                            ", 未知身份: " + str(names.count("Unknown")) if names.count("Unknown") else "",
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    self.append_text_and_scroll("启用人脸识别：无人脸")

        scale = min(0.75 * max(self.width(), 640) / frame.shape[1], 0.75 * max(self.height(), 480) / frame.shape[0])
        new_w = max(4, int(frame.shape[1] * scale) // 4 * 4)
        new_h = max(4, int(frame.shape[0] * scale) // 4 * 4)
        frame_scaled = cv2.resize(frame, (new_w, new_h))
        image = QImage(frame_scaled.data, frame_scaled.shape[1], frame_scaled.shape[0],
                       frame_scaled.strides[0], QImage.Format_BGR888)
        self.image_label.setPixmap(QPixmap.fromImage(image))

    def append_text_and_scroll(self, text):
        self.info_text.append(text)
        self.info_text.ensureCursorVisible()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    main_window = FaceWidget()
    main_window.show()
    sys.exit(app.exec_())
台架集成（虚拟座舱联动）
集成 1｜智能座舱虚拟台架/python/questions/logic.py

个人学生版的座舱有 data/seat_memory.ini，但没有教师版使用的 face_profiles.py。本课就在个人副本的 智能座舱虚拟台架/python/questions/logic.py 用短函数读取现成 INI；不重写注册系统。先把下面两行放在原文件顶部的 from module_bridge import CourseModules 后，再完成已有的 send_seat()、read_face_profile()、handle_face_name() 三个占位函数。这里给接口、协议字段与边界，完整实现只留在教师标准答案。

【座舱集成第 1 步（logic.py & seat_memory.ini）：核心填空 + 参数扩展与个人标识】：① 在个人副本 智能座舱虚拟台架/data/seat_memory.ini 末尾核对/补全 [user_temp] 分节（rail = 210、backrest = 100，全班统一参数）；并在台架返回提示中附带个人姓名或学号；② 将下方带 4 处 ____ 空位 的代码替换进 logic.py（补全 SEAT、"default"、read_face_profile、LEFT）。参数扩展要求：在 seat_memory.ini [user_temp] 修改并对比另一组 rail/backrest 迎宾位姿变化后恢复原值，确保台架界面反馈包含个人姓名或学号。

查看代码Python
from pathlib import Path
import configparser

def send_seat(side, rail, backrest):
    """发送智能座椅滑轨与靠背目标位置协议帧（请补全 1 处 ____ 空位）"""
    frame = make_frame(
        device=____, side=side, value=MOVE_TO,
        p1=int(rail), p2=MOVE_TO, p3=int(backrest),
    )
    send_frame(frame)
    return f"座椅目标：滑轨 {rail}，靠背 {backrest}"

def read_face_profile(name):
    """读取 seat_memory.ini 中的对应姓名座椅档案，未知身份回退 default（请补全 1 处 ____ 空位）"""
    ini_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "data" / "seat_memory.ini"
    config = configparser.ConfigParser()
    if not config.read(ini_path, encoding="utf-8"):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"找不到座椅档案：{ini_path}")
    section = name if name != "Unknown" and config.has_section(name) else ____
    if not config.has_section(section):
        raise ValueError("座椅档案缺少 default 分节")
    return config.getint(section, "rail"), config.getint(section, "backrest")

def handle_face_name(name):
    """根据识别出的姓名触发主驾（LEFT）座椅自动迎宾调节（请补全 2 处 ____ 空位）"""
    if not name:
        return "未检测到人脸"
    rail, backrest = ____(name)
    return send_seat(____, rail, backrest)
函数 你需要完成的连接 不能漏掉的边界
send_seat(side, rail, backrest) 用已有 make_frame() 生成座椅帧，再 send_frame(frame)；device=SEAT、side=side、value=MOVE_TO、p1=int(rail)、p2=MOVE_TO、p3=int(backrest) 返回包含滑轨与靠背目标值的文字；不手拼 28 字节帧
read_face_profile(name) 用 Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "data" / "seat_memory.ini" 定位文件，configparser.ConfigParser() 读取；姓名分节存在时用姓名，否则用 default 文件缺失或缺少 default 要提示；返回所选分节的两个整数 rail、backrest
handle_face_name(name) 有姓名时读取档案并调用 send_seat(LEFT, rail, backrest) 空姓名只提示“未检测到人脸”，不发帧；Unknown 必须走 default

可以在原三个函数签名下按上表编写，函数名与参数不要改。座舱协议工具已在原项目中，不复制座椅台架的 make_packet() 到座舱。

打开个人副本的 智能座舱虚拟台架/data/seat_memory.ini，保留已有 [default]。如果 C3 已成功生成 user_temp 特征，在文件末尾加入以下分节，用于观察已知姓名与默认档案的不同座椅位置：

查看代码INI
[user_temp]
rail = 210
backrest = 100

未生成有效特征时，保留真实 Unknown 结果，并使用已有的 [default]；不要按照片文件名直接返回 user_temp。

集成 2｜智能座舱虚拟台架/python/questions/control_center.py

修改 face_page(self)：把原来仅处理文字身份的占位页面改为固定图片入口。输入框接收图片绝对路径；用 cv2.imread() 读图，读不到要返回错误；成功时调用 logic.modules.face(frame)，它返回标注图、人脸框、姓名列表。无人脸或无姓名时不发座椅帧；有姓名时取第一个真实姓名交给 logic.handle_face_name(name)，页面同时显示姓名与处理结果。其余页面和窗口布局不改。

【座舱集成第 2 步（control_center.py）：核心填空】：请将下方带 2 处 ____ 空位 的 face_page(self) 替换进 control_center.py（补全 face 与 handle_face_name）。

def face_page(self):
    """选择固定人脸图片 -> 识别身份姓名 -> 自动调节座椅记忆位置（请补全 2 处 ____ 空位）"""
    def handle_file(path):
        frame = cv2.imread(path.strip())
        if frame is None:
            return "图片读取失败：请检查绝对路径"
        shown, faces, names = logic.modules.____(frame)
        if not faces or not names:
            return "未检测到人脸或姓名"
        name = names[0]
        result = logic.____(name)
        return f"识别：{name}；处理：{result}"

    return BasicVisionPage("选择固定人脸图片，按身份调整座椅。", "粘贴图片绝对路径", handle_file)

任务 6.2

驾驶监控数据采集与标注

环节
驾驶监控数据采集与标注

驾驶监控数据采集与标注

使用说明

任务： 在没有摄像头的学生机上，使用个人解压副本里的固定图完成数据筛选、标签记录、目标框标注和互审。只新建自己的标注结果，不改 A/B/C/D 脚本和原始压缩包。教师已给出第一行示例，其余由你补齐。

一、物体检测数据筛选
数据

筛选目标　区分目标类别、图像清晰度和是否需要复核。

步骤 操作 当场检查
1 打开三张图，确认正常、水瓶、模糊手机各是什么样 三张真实图片找对

5 保存并截图CSV，交给教师 labels.csv 能重新打开

object_label 只填 none、bottle、cell_phone；quality 只填 clear 或 blur；decision 只填 keep 或 review。review 表示暂待复核，不等于已经删除。图中看见水瓶只说明“有水瓶目标”，不能直接写“驾驶员正在喝水”。

A1｜questions/fatigue_detector/A/A1.py

用下方完整代码稿替换个人副本 A1.py，在固定图片上检测人脸并输出 68 个关键点坐标。

【运行任务】：下方直接给出完整 A1.py 代码，替换个人副本后直接运行，查看 68 个关键点提取与标注结果。

查看代码Python
"""任务A1：人脸关键点检测

独立的人脸关键点检测脚本。
功能：
1. 读取指定路径的图像文件，检测图像中的人脸。
2. 使用dlib提取人脸的68个关键点（每个关键点的(x, y)坐标）。
3. 在控制台打印所有关键点的编号和坐标。
4. 将关键点坐标保存到txt文件，每一行保存一个关键点的编号和坐标。
5. 可视化：在图像上绘制关键点并显示图像
"""
import os
import cv2
import dlib
import imutils
import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import distance as dist
from imutils import face_utils

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        """dlib的初始化调用"""
        # 使用人脸检测器get_frontal_face_detector
        self.detector = dlib.get_frontal_face_detector()
        # dlib的68点模型，使用作者训练好的特征预测器
        self.predictor = dlib.shape_predictor("../data/models/shape_predictor_68_face_landmarks.dat")

    def detect_face(self, frame):
        # 图像对象，图像的三维矩阵
        frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)
        # 取灰度
        img_gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY)
        # 使用人脸检测器检测每一帧图像中的人脸。并返回人脸数faces
        rects = self.detector(img_gray, 0)
        all_landmarks = []
        if len(rects) > 0:
            print(f"检测到 {len(rects)} 个人脸")
            cv2.putText(frame, "Faces: {}".format(len(rects)), (10, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
            # 存储所有检测到的人脸关键点坐标
            for id, rect in enumerate(rects):
                # 用绿色矩形框出人脸
                cv2.rectangle(frame, (rect.left(), rect.top()), (rect.right(), rect.bottom()), (0, 255, 0), 3)
                # 使用预测器得到68点数据的坐标
                shape = self.predictor(frame, rect)
                # 将脸部特征信息转换为数组array的格式
                shape = face_utils.shape_to_np(shape)

                print(f"人脸 {id + 1} 的关键点坐标：")
                face_landmarks = []
                for i, (x, y) in enumerate(shape, start=1):
                    # 圆圈显示每个特征点
                    cv2.circle(frame, (x, y), 1, (0, 0, 255), -1)
                    # 打印当前人脸所有关键点的编号和坐标
                    print(f"  {i}: ({x}, {y})")
                    face_landmarks.append((x, y))
                all_landmarks.append(face_landmarks)
        else:
            cv2.putText(frame, "No Face", (20, 50), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3, cv2.LINE_AA)

        return frame, all_landmarks

def main():
    # 读取图像
    image_path = "../data/images/12615.jpg"
    frame = cv2.imread(image_path)
    if frame is None:
        print(f"Error: 未能读取图像，请检查路径是否正确: {image_path}")
        return

    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    frame, faces = monitor.detect_face(frame)

    # 可视化：显示标注了关键点的图像
    cv2.imshow("人脸关键点绘制结果", frame)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

    # 将关键点坐标保存到txt文件，每一行：landmark_index x y
    output_file = "../output/face_landmarks.txt"
    os.makedirs("../output", exist_ok=True)
    with open(output_file, 'w') as f:
        for face_idx, face_landmarks in enumerate(faces):
            f.write(f"Face {face_idx}:\n")
            for id, (x, y) in enumerate(face_landmarks):
                f.write(f"{id + 1} {x} {y}\n")

    print(f"关键点坐标已保存到: {output_file}")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

运行验证： 以 questions/fatigue_detector/A 为工作目录运行 A1.py，核对终端输出的 68 个点坐标与画点结果。

A2｜questions/fatigue_detector/A/A2.py

用下方完整代码稿替换个人副本 A2.py，结合 68 点索引实现 mouth_aspect_ratio() 与 eye_aspect_ratio() 开度计算。

【填空任务】：补全 A2.py 的 2 处 ____ 空位（嘴部横向距离与 MAR 分母），运行查看 MAR 与 EAR 实测数值。

查看代码Python
"""任务A2：张嘴睁眼幅度计算

张嘴/睁眼幅度判断与结果保存。
功能：
1. 读取关键点坐标（txt）
2. 嘴巴开合度计算
3. 眼睛睁闭度计算
4. 多帧处理，输出全部帧判断结果
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import distance as dist

import cv2
import imutils
from imutils import face_utils

import os

def mouth_aspect_ratio(mouth):
    vertical_distance_1 = np.linalg.norm(mouth[2] - mouth[10])  # 51, 59
    vertical_distance_2 = np.linalg.norm(mouth[4] - mouth[8])  # 53, 57
    horizontal_distance = np.linalg.norm(____)  # 49, 55
    mar = (vertical_distance_1 + vertical_distance_2) / (2.0 * ____)
    return mar

def eye_aspect_ratio(eye):
    # 垂直眼标志（X，Y）坐标
    A = dist.euclidean(eye[1], eye[5])  # 计算两个集合之间的欧氏距离
    B = dist.euclidean(eye[2], eye[4])
    # 计算水平之间的欧几里得距离
    # 水平眼标志（X，Y）坐标
    C = dist.euclidean(eye[0], eye[3])
    # 眼睛长宽比的计算
    ear = (A + B) / (2.0 * C)
    # 返回眼睛的长宽比
    return ear

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        # 分别获取左右眼、嘴巴等面部标志的索引
        (self.lStart, self.lEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["left_eye"]
        (self.rStart, self.rEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["right_eye"]
        (self.mStart, self.mEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["mouth"]

    def detect_core(self, frame, faces):
        # 图像对象，图像的三维矩阵
        frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)

        # 嘴巴开合度/眼睛睁闭度/人脸数
        mar, ear, face_num = -1,-1, len(faces)
        if face_num > 0:
            cv2.putText(frame, "Faces: {}".format(len(faces)), (10, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
            for shape in faces:
                """
                打哈欠
                """
                # 嘴巴坐标
                mouth = np.array(shape[self.mStart + 1:self.mEnd])
                # 打哈欠
                mar = mouth_aspect_ratio(mouth)
                # 使用cv.convexHull获得凸包位置，使用drawContours画出轮廓位置进行画图操作
                mouthHull = cv2.convexHull(mouth)
                cv2.drawContours(frame, [mouthHull], -1, (0, 255, 0), 1)
                cv2.putText(frame, "MAR: {:.2f}".format(mar), (480, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)

                """
                眨眼
                """
                # 提取左眼和右眼坐标
                leftEye = np.array(shape[self.lStart:self.lEnd])
                rightEye = np.array(shape[self.rStart:self.rEnd])
                # 构造函数计算左右眼的EAR值，使用平均值作为最终的EAR
                leftEAR = eye_aspect_ratio(leftEye)
                rightEAR = eye_aspect_ratio(rightEye)
                ear = (leftEAR + rightEAR) / 2.0
                leftEyeHull = cv2.convexHull(leftEye)
                rightEyeHull = cv2.convexHull(rightEye)
                # 使用cv2.convexHull获得凸包位置，使用drawContours画出轮廓位置进行画图操作
                cv2.drawContours(frame, [leftEyeHull], -1, (0, 255, 0), 1)
                cv2.drawContours(frame, [rightEyeHull], -1, (0, 255, 0), 1)
                cv2.putText(frame, "EAR: {:.2f}".format(ear), (480, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
        else:
            cv2.putText(frame, "No Face", (20, 50), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3, cv2.LINE_AA)

        return frame, [mar, ear, face_num]

def parser_landmarks(landmarks):
    faces = []
    with open(landmarks, "r") as f:
        current_face = []
        for line in f.readlines():
            line = line.strip()
            if line.startswith("Face"):
                if current_face:
                    faces.append(current_face)
                    current_face = []
            elif line:
                _, x, y = line.strip().split()
                current_face.append((int(x), int(y)))
        if current_face:
            faces.append(current_face)
    return faces

def process_single_frame(image_path, input_file, output_file):
    # 读取图像
    frame = cv2.imread(image_path)
    if frame is None:
        print(f"Error: 未能读取图像，请检查路径是否正确: {image_path}")
        return

    # 解析关键点文件
    faces = parser_landmarks(input_file)

    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    frame, result = monitor.detect_core(frame, faces)

    cv2.imshow("嘴巴可视化结果", frame)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_file) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    with open(output_file, "w") as f:
        f.write(" ".join(map(str, result)) + "\n")
    print(f"[单帧张嘴判断] 结果: {result}")

# === 多帧处理 ===
def process_multiple_frames(input_files, output_file):
    results = []
    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    for image_path in input_files:
        # 读取图像
        frame = cv2.imread(image_path)

        # 解析关键点文件
        faces_txt = os.path.join("../fixtures/frame_landmarks", os.path.basename(image_path).rsplit(".", 1)[0] + '.txt')
        faces = parser_landmarks(faces_txt)

        # 张嘴判断
        frame, result = monitor.detect_core(frame, faces)
        cv2.putText(frame, f"{image_path}", (10, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 255, 0), 2)
        results.append(result)

        cv2.imshow("嘴巴关键点可视化结果", frame)
        cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(output_file) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    with open(output_file, "w") as f:
        for result in results:
            f.write(" ".join(map(str, result)) + "\n")
    print(f"[多帧张嘴判断] 共处理 {len(results)} 帧，已保存至 {output_file}")

# === 主函数入口 ===
def main():
    # 任务1：单帧判断
    landmarks_file = "../output/face_landmarks.txt"
    if not os.path.isfile(landmarks_file) or not os.path.getsize(landmarks_file):
        landmarks_file = "../fixtures/face_landmarks.txt"
    process_single_frame("../data/images/12615.jpg", landmarks_file, "../output/open_result.txt")

    # 任务2：多帧判断
    input_folder = "../data/frames"
    base_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(input_folder) if f.endswith(".jpg")])
    input_files = [os.path.join(input_folder, f) for f in base_files]
    process_multiple_frames(input_files, "../output/open_result.txt")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

空位提示：

  • 空 1：嘴部横向距离用两个嘴角点 0、6 的差。
  • 空 2：MAR 的分母是两倍嘴宽。

def eye_aspect_ratio(eye):

    pass

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        # TODO：请直接复用/整合你在 A1.py 完成的 dlib 检测器与 mouth/left_eye/right_eye 索引初始化
        self.detector = dlib.get_frontal_face_detector()
        self.predictor = dlib.shape_predictor(____)
        (self.mStart, self.mEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["mouth"]
        (self.lStart, self.lEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["left_eye"]
        (self.rStart, self.rEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["right_eye"]

    def detect_core(self, frame):
        frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        rects = self.detector(gray, 0)
        mar, ear = 0.0, 0.0
        for rect in rects:
            shape = face_utils.shape_to_np(self.predictor(gray, rect))
            # TODO：复用 A2.py 的绿色多边形轮廓绘制，并在原状态文字中附带个人学号
            mar = ____(shape[self.mStart:self.mEnd])
            ear = (____(shape[self.lStart:self.lEnd]) + eye_aspect_ratio(shape[self.rStart:self.rEnd])) / 2.0
        cv2.putText(frame, f"MAR:{mar:.2f} EAR:{ear:.2f} | ID:<xuehao>", (20, 35),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 255, 255), 2)
        return frame, [mar, ear, len(rects)]

def main():
    image = cv2.imread("../data/images/12615.jpg")
    if image is None:
        print("Error: 未能读取图像 ../data/images/12615.jpg")
        return
    detector = FatigueDetector()
    frame, result = detector.detect_core(image)
    print(f"[单帧张嘴判断] 学生：姓名/学号 | 结果: {result}")
    os.makedirs("../output", exist_ok=True)
    with open("../output/open_result.txt", "w") as f:
        f.write(" ".join(map(str, result)) + "\n")
    cv2.imshow("人脸关键点绘制结果", frame)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

B1｜questions/fatigue_detector/B/B1.py

用下方完整代码稿替换个人副本 B1.py，读取 ../fixtures/open_result.txt 验证连续张嘴帧与哈欠事件计数。

【填空任务】：补全 B1.py 的 2 组（共 4 处）____ 空位（张嘴判定比较符与帧计数器自增），运行核对哈欠计数输出。

查看代码Python
"""任务B1：打哈欠次数统计"""

import cv2
import imutils
import os

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        # 初始化打哈欠帧计数器和打哈欠总数
        self.mCOUNTER = 0  # 连续判断为打哈欠的帧数
        self.mTOTAL = 0  # 打哈欠次数
        self.mLIST = list()  # 打哈欠历史信息

        # 嘴巴参数阈值
        self.MAR_THRESH = 0.8
        self.MOU_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES = 20

    def detect_core(self, frame, result):
        # 图像对象，图像的三维矩阵
        frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)

        # 嘴巴开合度/眼睛睁闭度/人脸数量
        mar, _, face_num = result
        # 疲劳状态初始化
        h = 0
        if face_num > 0:
            # 循环，满足条件的，打哈欠次数+1
            # 判断是否打哈欠
            if mar ____ self.MAR_THRESH:
                self.mCOUNTER += ____
                self.mLIST += [0]
            else:
                # 如果连续3次都大于阈值，则表示打了一次哈欠
                if self.mCOUNTER >= self.MOU_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES:  # 阈值：3
                    self.mTOTAL += 1
                    self.mLIST += [1]
                    # 显示
                    h = 1
                    cv2.putText(frame, "Yawning!", (10, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
                else:
                    self.mLIST += [0]
                # 重置嘴帧计数器
                self.mCOUNTER = 0
            # 进行画图操作，同时使用cv2.putText将打哈欠次数进行显示
            cv2.putText(frame, "Yawning: {}".format(self.mTOTAL), (150, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
            cv2.putText(frame, "mCOUNTER: {}".format(self.mCOUNTER), (300, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)

        return frame, h

def gen_input_files(input_folder, filter='.jpg'):
    base_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(input_folder) if f.endswith(filter)])
    input_files = [os.path.join(input_folder, f) for f in base_files]
    return input_files

def main():
    results = []
    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    image_folder = gen_input_files("../data/frames")
    result_file = "../fixtures/open_result.txt"
    generated_file = "../output/open_result.txt"
    if os.path.isfile(generated_file):
        with open(generated_file, "r") as generated:
            if sum(1 for _ in generated) == len(image_folder):
                result_file = generated_file
    with open(result_file, "r") as f:
        for image_path, result in zip(image_folder, f.readlines()):
            # 读取图像
            frame = cv2.imread(image_path)
            state = list(map(float, result.strip().split()))

            frame, result = monitor.detect_core(frame, state)
            cv2.putText(frame, f"{image_path}", (10, 360), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 255, 0), 2)
            results.append(result)

            cv2.imshow("打哈欠次数可视化结果", frame)
            if cv2.waitKey(50) & 0xFF == ord("q"):
                break
        cv2.destroyAllWindows()

    output_file = "../output/fatigue_yawning_result.txt"
    os.makedirs("../output", exist_ok=True)
    with open(output_file, "w") as f:
        for result in results:
            f.write(str(result) + "\n")
    print(f"[多帧打哈欠次数判断] 共处理 {len(results)} 帧，已保存至 {output_file}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

空位提示：

  • 空 1：张嘴比值超过阈值时开始计数。
  • 空 2：满足阈值的每帧把连续计数加一。
B2｜questions/fatigue_detector/B/B2.py

用下方完整代码稿替换个人副本 B2.py，读取 ../fixtures/open_result.txt 验证连续闭眼帧与眨眼事件计数。

【填空任务】：补全 B2.py 的 2 组（共 3 处）____ 空位（闭眼判定比较符与帧计数器自增），运行核对眨眼计数输出。

查看代码Python
"""任务B2：眨眼次数统计"""

import cv2
import imutils
import os

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        # 初始化眨眼帧计数器和眨眼总数
        self.COUNTER = 0  # 连续判断为眨眼的帧数
        self.TOTAL = 0  # 眨眼次数
        self.LIST = list()  # 眨眼历史信息

        # 眼睛参数阈值
        self.EAR_THRESH = 0.25
        self.EYE_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES = 10

    def detect_core(self, frame, result):
        # 图像对象，图像的三维矩阵
        frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)

        # 嘴巴开合度/眼睛睁闭度/人脸数量
        _, ear, face_num = result
        # 疲劳状态初始化
        b = 0
        if face_num > 0:
            # 循环，满足条件的，眨眼次数+1
            # 判断眼睛是否闭上
            if ear ____ self.EAR_THRESH:  # 眼睛长宽比阈值（约 0.25）
                self.COUNTER += ____
                self.LIST += [0]
            else:
                # 如果连续3次都小于阈值，则表示进行了一次眨眼活动
                if self.COUNTER >= self.EYE_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES:  # 阈值：3
                    self.TOTAL += 1
                    self.LIST += [1]
                    # 显示
                    b = 1
                    cv2.putText(frame, "Blinks!", (10, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
                else:
                    self.LIST += [0]
                # 重置眼帧计数器
                self.COUNTER = 0
            # 进行画图操作，同时使用cv2.putText将眨眼次数进行显示
            cv2.putText(frame, "Blinks: {}".format(self.TOTAL), (150, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
            cv2.putText(frame, "COUNTER: {}".format(self.COUNTER), (300, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)

        return frame, b

def gen_input_files(input_folder, filter=".jpg"):
    base_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(input_folder) if f.endswith(filter)])
    input_files = [os.path.join(input_folder, f) for f in base_files]
    return input_files

def main():
    results = []
    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    image_folder = gen_input_files("../data/frames")
    result_file = "../fixtures/open_result.txt"
    generated_file = "../output/open_result.txt"
    if os.path.isfile(generated_file):
        with open(generated_file, "r") as generated:
            if sum(1 for _ in generated) == len(image_folder):
                result_file = generated_file
    with open(result_file, "r") as f:
        for image_path, result in zip(image_folder, f.readlines()):
            # 读取图像
            frame = cv2.imread(image_path)
            state = list(map(float, result.strip().split()))

            frame, result = monitor.detect_core(frame, state)
            cv2.putText(frame, f"{image_path}", (10, 360), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 255, 0), 2)
            results.append(result)

            cv2.imshow("眨眼次数可视化结果", frame)
            if cv2.waitKey(50) & 0xFF == ord("q"):
                break
        cv2.destroyAllWindows()

    output_file = "../output/fatigue_blinks_result.txt"
    os.makedirs("../output", exist_ok=True)
    with open(output_file, "w") as f:
        for result in results:
            f.write(str(result) + "\n")
    print(f"[多帧眨眼次数判断] 共处理 {len(results)} 帧，已保存至 {output_file}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

  • 空 2：满足阈值的每帧把闭眼连续计数加一。

【填空任务】：补全 C1.py 的 2 处 ____ 空位（哈欠窗口阈值与比较判断），对比正常与触发样本输出。

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""任务C1：打哈欠判定与报警"""
import cv2
import imutils
import os

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        # 初始化打哈欠帧计数器和打哈欠总数
        self.mTOTAL = 0  # 打哈欠次数
        self.mLIST = list()  # 打哈欠历史信息

        # 嘴巴参数阈值
        self.MOU_FR = ____

        # 报警参数阈值
        self.ALERTS_FRAMES = 100  # 警报阈值计数器，达到阈值播放警报音频（建议设置为150）

def gen_input_files(input_folder, filter='.jpg'):
    base_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(input_folder) if f.endswith(filter)])
    input_files = [os.path.join(input_folder, f) for f in base_files]
    return input_files

if __name__ == '__main__':
    nshow = 0
    mring_counter = 0

    # 疲劳检测
    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    image_folder = gen_input_files("../data/frames")
    result_file = "../fixtures/fatigue_result.txt"
    generated_file = "../output/fatigue_result.txt"
    if os.path.isfile(generated_file):
        with open(generated_file, "r") as generated:
            if sum(1 for _ in generated) == len(image_folder):
                result_file = generated_file
    with open(result_file, "r") as f:
        for image_path, result in zip(image_folder, f.readlines()):
            # 读取图像
            frame = cv2.imread(image_path)
            frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)

            fatigue = list(map(int, result.strip().split()))
            monitor.mLIST.append(fatigue[0])
            if len(monitor.mLIST) >= monitor.ALERTS_FRAMES:
                monitor.mLIST = monitor.mLIST[1:]

            if fatigue[0]: monitor.mTOTAL += 1
            cv2.putText(frame, f"Yawning: {monitor.mTOTAL} / Valid Yawning: {sum(monitor.mLIST)}",
                        (300, 100), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3)

            if sum(monitor.mLIST) >= ____:
                if mring_counter == 0:
                    mring_counter += 1
                    print("疲劳提醒")
                    nshow = 100
                    for _ in range(len(monitor.mLIST)):
                        monitor.mLIST[_] = 0
            else:
                mring_counter = 0

            # 按q退出
            if nshow > 0:
                nshow = nshow - 1
                cv2.putText(frame, "SLEEPING!!!", (100, 200), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3)
            cv2.putText(frame, "Press 'q': Quit", (20, 500), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (84, 255, 159), 2)

            cv2.imshow('Fatigue Detector', frame)
            cv2.moveWindow("Fatigue Detector", 100, 100)

            if cv2.waitKey(25) == ord('q'):
                break

        cv2.destroyAllWindows()

class FatigueDetector:

        # 初始化眨眼帧计数器和眨眼总数
        self.TOTAL = 0  # 眨眼次数
        self.LIST = list()  # 眨眼历史信息

        # 眼睛参数阈值
        self.EYE_FR = ____

        # 报警参数阈值
        self.ALERTS_FRAMES = 100  # 警报阈值计数器，达到阈值播放警报音频（建议设置为150）

def gen_input_files(input_folder, filter='.jpg'):
    base_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(input_folder) if f.endswith(filter)])
    input_files = [os.path.join(input_folder, f) for f in base_files]
    return input_files

if __name__ == '__main__':
    nshow = 0
    mring_counter = 0

    # 疲劳检测
    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    image_folder = gen_input_files("../data/frames")
    result_file = "../fixtures/fatigue_result.txt"
    generated_file = "../output/fatigue_result.txt"
    if os.path.isfile(generated_file):
        with open(generated_file, "r") as generated:
            if sum(1 for _ in generated) == len(image_folder):
                result_file = generated_file
    with open(result_file, "r") as f:
        for image_path, result in zip(image_folder, f.readlines()):
            # 读取图像
            frame = cv2.imread(image_path)
            frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)

            fatigue = list(map(int, result.strip().split()))
            monitor.LIST.append(fatigue[1])
            if len(monitor.LIST) >= monitor.ALERTS_FRAMES:
                monitor.LIST = monitor.LIST[1:]

            if fatigue[1]: monitor.TOTAL += 1
            cv2.putText(frame, f"Blinks: {monitor.TOTAL} / Valid Blinks: {sum(monitor.LIST)}",
                        (300, 100), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3)

            if sum(monitor.LIST) >= ____:
                if mring_counter == 0:
                    mring_counter += 1
                    print("疲劳提醒")
                    nshow = 100
                    for _ in range(len(monitor.LIST)):
                        monitor.LIST[_] = 0
            else:
                mring_counter = 0

            # 按q退出
            if nshow > 0:
                nshow = nshow - 1
                cv2.putText(frame, "SLEEPING!!!", (100, 200), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3)
            cv2.putText(frame, "Press 'q': Quit", (20, 500), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (84, 255, 159), 2)

            cv2.imshow('Fatigue Detector', frame)
            cv2.moveWindow("Fatigue Detector", 100, 100)

            if cv2.waitKey(25) == ord('q'):
                break

        cv2.destroyAllWindows()

空位提示：

  • 空 1：教师版历史窗口里两个眨眼事件触发提醒。
  • 空 2：与当前对象的眨眼触发次数阈值比较。

D 组（驾驶疲劳检测）代码说明
D 组运行支撑｜questions/fatigue_detector/fatigue_helpers.py

学生包这里的 MAR、EAR、setPM()、setPE() 尚是空实现；先把下面与教师版一致的完整代码放入个人副本同名文件，D1～D3 才能运行。这段是已给支撑代码，不另设填空。

【前置支撑文件（直接复制）】：请先将下方完整的 fatigue_helpers.py 复制到根目录 questions/fatigue_detector/fatigue_helpers.py，供 D1～D3 调用。

查看代码Python
"""疲劳检测辅助函数"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.spatial import distance as dist

def mouth_aspect_ratio(mouth):
    """根据嘴部关键点计算MAR"""
    vertical_distance_1 = np.linalg.norm(mouth[2] - mouth[10])  # 51, 59
    vertical_distance_2 = np.linalg.norm(mouth[4] - mouth[8])  # 53, 57
    horizontal_distance = np.linalg.norm(mouth[0] - mouth[6])  # 49, 55
    mar = (vertical_distance_1 + vertical_distance_2) / (2.0 * horizontal_distance)
    return mar

def eye_aspect_ratio(eye):
    """根据眼部关键点计算EAR"""
    # 垂直眼标志（X，Y）坐标
    A = dist.euclidean(eye[1], eye[5])  # 计算两个集合之间的欧氏距离
    B = dist.euclidean(eye[2], eye[4])
    # 计算水平之间的欧几里得距离
    # 水平眼标志（X，Y）坐标
    C = dist.euclidean(eye[0], eye[3])
    # 眼睛长宽比的计算
    ear = (A + B) / (2.0 * C)
    # 返回眼睛的长宽比
    return ear

def setPM():
    """返回MAR阈值、连续张嘴帧阈值和窗口事件阈值"""
    mar_thresh = 0.8  # 0.5
    mou_ar_consec_frames = 20  # 3
    mou_fr = 2

    return mar_thresh, mou_ar_consec_frames, mou_fr

def setPE():
    """返回EAR阈值、连续闭眼帧阈值和窗口事件阈值"""
    ear_thresh = 0.25  # 0.2
    eye_ar_consec_frames = 10  # 3
    eye_fr = 2

    return ear_thresh, eye_ar_consec_frames, eye_fr
D1｜questions/fatigue_detector/yawning_detector.py

用下方固定视频无声音版替换根目录 yawning_detector.py，调用 fatigue_helpers.py 完成嘴部哈欠连续视频检测。

【运行任务】：下方直接给出完整 yawning_detector.py 代码，覆盖根目录同名文件后直接运行，观察固定视频中的哈欠检测与文字告警。

查看代码Python
"""任务D1：打哈欠检测参数设置"""

import cv2
import dlib
import imutils
from imutils import face_utils

from fatigue_helpers import *

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        """dlib的初始化调用"""
        # 使用人脸检测器get_frontal_face_detector
        self.detector = dlib.get_frontal_face_detector()
        # dlib的68点模型，使用作者训练好的特征预测器
        self.predictor = dlib.shape_predictor("./data/models/shape_predictor_68_face_landmarks.dat")

        # 分别获取嘴巴标志的索引
        (self.mStart, self.mEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["mouth"]

        # 初始化打哈欠帧计数器和打哈欠总数
        self.mCOUNTER = 0  # 连续判断为打哈欠的帧数
        self.mTOTAL = 0  # 打哈欠次数
        self.mLIST = list()  # 打哈欠历史信息

        # 初始化离岗帧计数器
        self.oCOUNTER = 0  # 连续判断为离岗的帧数

        # 参数阈值
        self.MAR_THRESH, self.MOU_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES, self.MOU_FR = setPM()
        self.OUT_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES = 5
        self.ALERTS_FRAMES = 100

    def detect_core(self, frame):
        # 图像对象，图像的三维矩阵
        frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)
        # 取灰度
        img_gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY)
        # 使用人脸检测器检测每一帧图像中的人脸。并返回人脸数faces
        rects = self.detector(img_gray, 0)
        # 疲劳状态初始化
        h = b = n = o = 0
        if len(rects) > 0:
            cv2.putText(frame, "Faces: {}".format(len(rects)), (10, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
            for rect in rects:
                # 用绿色矩形框出人脸
                cv2.rectangle(frame, (rect.left(), rect.top()), (rect.right(), rect.bottom()), (0, 255, 0), 3)
                # 使用预测器得到68点数据的坐标
                shape = self.predictor(frame, rect)
                # 将脸部特征信息转换为数组array的格式
                shape = face_utils.shape_to_np(shape)
                # 圆圈显示每个特征点
                for (x, y) in shape:
                    cv2.circle(frame, (x, y), 1, (0, 0, 255), -1)
                # 打哈欠判断
                # 嘴巴坐标
                mouth = shape[self.mStart + 1:self.mEnd]
                # 打哈欠
                mar = mouth_aspect_ratio(mouth)
                # 使用cv.convexHull获得凸包位置，使用drawContours画出轮廓位置进行画图操作
                mouthHull = cv2.convexHull(mouth)
                cv2.drawContours(frame, [mouthHull], -1, (0, 255, 0), 1)
                # 同理，判断是否打哈欠
                if mar > self.MAR_THRESH:  # 张嘴阈值0.5
                    self.mCOUNTER += 1
                    self.mLIST += [0]
                else:
                    # 如果连续3次都大于阈值，则表示打了一次哈欠
                    if self.mCOUNTER >= self.MOU_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES:  # 阈值：3
                        self.mTOTAL += 1
                        self.mLIST += [1]
                        # 显示
                        h = 1
                        cv2.putText(frame, "Yawning!", (10, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
                    else:
                        self.mLIST += [0]
                    # 重置嘴帧计数器
                    self.mCOUNTER = 0
                cv2.putText(frame, "Yawning: {}".format(self.mTOTAL), (150, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
                cv2.putText(frame, "mCOUNTER: {}".format(self.mCOUNTER), (300, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
                cv2.putText(frame, "MAR: {:.2f}".format(mar), (480, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
        else:
            self.oCOUNTER += 1
            cv2.putText(frame, "No Face", (20, 50), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3, cv2.LINE_AA)
            if self.oCOUNTER >= self.OUT_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES:
                o = 1
                self.oCOUNTER = 0

        return frame, [h, o]

if __name__ == '__main__':
    # 学生机无摄像头：读取包内固定视频
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture("./data/videos/11-MaleGlasses-Yawning.mp4")
    if not cap.isOpened():
        print("无法打开固定视频。")
        exit()

    nshow = 0
    mring_counter = 0

    # 疲劳检测
    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    while True:
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        if not ret:
            break
        frame = cv2.flip(frame, 1)

        frame, _ = monitor.detect_core(frame)
        if len(monitor.mLIST) >= monitor.ALERTS_FRAMES:
            monitor.mLIST = monitor.mLIST[1:]

        if sum(monitor.mLIST) >= monitor.MOU_FR:
            if mring_counter == 0:
                mring_counter += 1
                print("疲劳提醒")
                nshow = 100
                for _ in range(len(monitor.mLIST)):
                    monitor.mLIST[_] = 0
        else:
            mring_counter = 0

        # 按q退出
        if nshow > 0:
            nshow = nshow - 1
            cv2.putText(frame, "SLEEPING!!!", (100, 200), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3)
        cv2.putText(frame, "Press 'q': Quit", (20, 500), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (84, 255, 159), 2)

        cv2.imshow('Fatigue Detector', frame)
        cv2.moveWindow("Fatigue Detector", 100, 100)

        if cv2.waitKey(1) == ord('q'):
            break

    cap.release()
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

运行验证： 以 questions/fatigue_detector 为工作目录运行 yawning_detector.py，观察视频窗口中的嘴部轮廓、哈欠计数与文字提醒。

D2｜questions/fatigue_detector/blinks_detector.py

用下方固定视频无声音版替换根目录 blinks_detector.py，调用 fatigue_helpers.py 完成眼部眨眼连续视频检测。

【填空任务】：补全 blinks_detector.py 的 2 处 ____ 空位（眼部阈值函数 setPE 与闭眼判定比较符 <），运行查看视频窗口与文字报警。

查看代码Python
"""任务D2：眨眼检测参数设置"""

import cv2
import dlib
import imutils
from imutils import face_utils

from fatigue_helpers import *

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        """dlib的初始化调用"""
        # 使用人脸检测器get_frontal_face_detector
        self.detector = dlib.get_frontal_face_detector()
        # dlib的68点模型，使用作者训练好的特征预测器
        self.predictor = dlib.shape_predictor("./data/models/shape_predictor_68_face_landmarks.dat")

        # 分别获取左右眼标志的索引
        (self.lStart, self.lEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["left_eye"]
        (self.rStart, self.rEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["right_eye"]

        # 初始化眨眼帧计数器和眨眼总数
        self.COUNTER = 0  # 连续判断为眨眼的帧数
        self.TOTAL = 0  # 眨眼次数
        self.LIST = list()  # 眨眼历史信息

        # 初始化离岗帧计数器
        self.oCOUNTER = 0  # 连续判断为离岗的帧数

        # 参数阈值
        self.EAR_THRESH, self.EYE_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES, self.EYE_FR = ____()
        self.OUT_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES = 5
        self.ALERTS_FRAMES = 100

    def detect_core(self, frame):
        # 图像对象，图像的三维矩阵
        frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)
        # 取灰度
        img_gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_RGB2GRAY)
        # 使用人脸检测器检测每一帧图像中的人脸。并返回人脸数faces
        rects = self.detector(img_gray, 0)
        # 疲劳状态初始化
        b = o = 0
        if len(rects) > 0:
            cv2.putText(frame, "Faces: {}".format(len(rects)), (10, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
            for rect in rects:
                # 用绿色矩形框出人脸
                cv2.rectangle(frame, (rect.left(), rect.top()), (rect.right(), rect.bottom()), (0, 255, 0), 3)
                # 使用预测器得到68点数据的坐标
                shape = self.predictor(frame, rect)
                # 将脸部特征信息转换为数组array的格式
                shape = face_utils.shape_to_np(shape)
                # 圆圈显示每个特征点
                for (x, y) in shape:
                    cv2.circle(frame, (x, y), 1, (0, 0, 255), -1)

                # 眨眼判断
                # 提取左眼和右眼坐标
                leftEye = shape[self.lStart:self.lEnd]
                rightEye = shape[self.rStart:self.rEnd]
                # 构造函数计算左右眼的EAR值，使用平均值作为最终的EAR
                leftEAR = eye_aspect_ratio(leftEye)
                rightEAR = eye_aspect_ratio(rightEye)
                ear = (leftEAR + rightEAR) / 2.0
                leftEyeHull = cv2.convexHull(leftEye)
                rightEyeHull = cv2.convexHull(rightEye)
                # 使用cv2.convexHull获得凸包位置，使用drawContours画出轮廓位置进行画图操作
                cv2.drawContours(frame, [leftEyeHull], -1, (0, 255, 0), 1)
                cv2.drawContours(frame, [rightEyeHull], -1, (0, 255, 0), 1)
                # 循环，满足条件的，眨眼次数+1
                if ear ____ self.EAR_THRESH:  # 眼睛长宽比：0.2
                    self.COUNTER += 1
                    self.LIST += [0]
                else:
                    # 如果连续3次都小于阈值，则表示进行了一次眨眼活动
                    if self.COUNTER >= self.EYE_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES:  # 阈值：3
                        self.TOTAL += 1
                        self.LIST += [1]
                        # 显示
                        b = 1
                        cv2.putText(frame, "Blinks!", (10, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
                    else:
                        self.LIST += [0]
                    # 重置眼帧计数器
                    self.COUNTER = 0
                # 进行画图操作，同时使用cv2.putText将眨眼次数进行显示
                cv2.putText(frame, "Blinks: {}".format(self.TOTAL), (150, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
                cv2.putText(frame, "COUNTER: {}".format(self.COUNTER), (300, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)
                cv2.putText(frame, "EAR: {:.2f}".format(ear), (480, 30), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)

        else:
            self.oCOUNTER += 1
            cv2.putText(frame, "No Face", (20, 50), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3, cv2.LINE_AA)
            if self.oCOUNTER >= self.OUT_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES:
                o = 1
                self.oCOUNTER = 0

        return frame, [b, o]

if __name__ == '__main__':
    # 学生机无摄像头：读取包内固定视频
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture("./data/videos/11-MaleGlasses-Yawning.mp4")
    if not cap.isOpened():
        print("无法打开固定视频。")
        exit()

    nshow = 0
    mring_counter = 0

    # 疲劳检测
    monitor = FatigueDetector()
    while True:
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        if not ret:
            break
        frame = cv2.flip(frame, 1)

        frame, _ = monitor.detect_core(frame)
        if len(monitor.LIST) >= monitor.ALERTS_FRAMES:
            monitor.LIST = monitor.LIST[1:]

        if sum(monitor.LIST) >= monitor.EYE_FR:
            if mring_counter == 0:
                mring_counter += 1
                print("疲劳提醒")
                nshow = 100
                for _ in range(len(monitor.LIST)):
                    monitor.LIST[_] = 0
        else:
            mring_counter = 0

        # 按q退出
        if nshow > 0:
            nshow = nshow - 1
            cv2.putText(frame, "SLEEPING!!!", (100, 200), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 3)
        cv2.putText(frame, "Press 'q': Quit", (20, 500), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (84, 255, 159), 2)

        cv2.imshow('Fatigue Detector', frame)
        cv2.moveWindow("Fatigue Detector", 100, 100)

        if cv2.waitKey(1) == ord('q'):
            break

    cap.release()
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

空位提示：

  • 空 1：眼部阈值由共用辅助文件里的 setPE 返回。
  • 空 2：EAR 低于眼部阈值时累计连续闭眼帧。

D1（yawning_detector.py）和 D2（blinks_detector.py）已完成固定视频下的单分支检测与计数器管理，通用特征与阈值函数已统一放在 fatigue_helpers.py。任务卡不再重复粘贴两百余行重复代码，请直接导入 fatigue_helpers 并整合 D1、D2 的打哈欠/眨眼事件计数与历史窗口逻辑，补全下方 5 处 ____ 空位 与视频画面个人学号绘制。

【综合任务 + 参数对照与个人标识】：用下方固定视频无声卡整合框架覆盖根目录 questions/fatigue_detector/fatigue_detector.py（D3），补全 5 处 ____ 空位（setPM、setPE、mTOTAL, TOTAL, out）。参数对照要求：对比单分支开关 use_mouth=True, use_eye=False 与双分支开关 use_mouth=True, use_eye=True 统计变化；在视频帧绘制个人学号（或姓名拼音），控制台报警输出个人姓名与学号。

"""任务D3：疲劳检测与报警（整合 D1 打哈欠分支与 D2 眨眼分支）"""
import cv2
import dlib
import imutils
from imutils import face_utils
from fatigue_helpers import eye_aspect_ratio, mouth_aspect_ratio, setPE, setPM

class FatigueDetector:
    def __init__(self):
        self.detector = dlib.get_frontal_face_detector()
        self.predictor = dlib.shape_predictor("./data/models/shape_predictor_68_face_landmarks.dat")
        (self.mStart, self.mEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["mouth"]
        (self.lStart, self.lEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["left_eye"]
        (self.rStart, self.rEnd) = face_utils.FACIAL_LANDMARKS_IDXS["right_eye"]
        # 调用 fatigue_helpers 统一初始化口部/眼部阈值与历史窗口上限
        self.MAR_THRESH, self.MOU_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES, self.MOU_FR = ____()
        self.EAR_THRESH, self.EYE_AR_CONSEC_FRAMES, self.EYE_FR = ____()
        self.mCOUNTER = self.mTOTAL = 0
        self.COUNTER = self.TOTAL = 0
        self.mLIST, self.LIST = [], []
        self.ALERTS_FRAMES = 100

    def detect_core(self, frame, use_mouth=True, use_eye=False):
        frame = imutils.resize(frame, width=720)
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        rects = self.detector(gray, 0)
        out = 0
        # TODO：请直接复用/整合你在 D1（yawning_detector.py）与 D2（blinks_detector.py）完成的单分支检测与事件计数逻辑：
        # 1) 对检测到的 rects[0] 计算 mar、ear；2) use_mouth=True 时更新打哈欠计数；3) use_eye=True 时更新眨眼计数；4) 无人脸则 out = 1
        cv2.putText(frame, f"Yawn:{self.mTOTAL} Blink:{self.TOTAL} ID:<xuehao>", (20, 35),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 255, 255), 2)
        return frame, [____, ____, ____]

if __name__ == "__main__":
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture("data/videos/11-MaleGlasses-Yawning.mp4")
    detector = FatigueDetector()
    frame_cnt = 0
    while cap.isOpened() and frame_cnt < 120:
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        if not ret:
            break
        frame_cnt += 1
        out_frame, status = detector.detect_core(frame, use_mouth=True, use_eye=True)
        if status[0] >= detector.MOU_FR or status[1] >= detector.EYE_FR:
            print(f"[疲劳提醒] 学生：姓名/学号 | 帧={frame_cnt}, 状态={status}")
        cv2.imshow("Fatigue Detector (Fixed Video)", out_frame)
        if cv2.waitKey(1) & 0xFF == ord("q"):
            break
    cap.release()
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

# 安装torch并验证是否安装成功
# pip install torch==2.4.0 torchvision==0.19.0

try:
    import torch

    print(f"PyTorch Version: {torch.__version__}")

    # 检查CUDA是否可用
    cuda_available = torch.cuda.is_available()
    print(f"CUDA Available: {cuda_available}")

    if cuda_available:
        # 打印出可用的GPU数量
        print(f"Number of GPUs: {torch.cuda.device_count()}")
        # 打印出当前默认的GPU设备名称

"""任务A2：目标识别模型测试"""

import os
import torch
import torchvision
from torchvision.transforms import functional as F
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

def object_detection_test():
    """
    执行目标检测并可视化结果
    """
    # 创建不下载权重的Faster R-CNN模型，用于验证输入输出结构
    model = torchvision.models.detection.____(
        weights=None, weights_backbone=None
    )
    model.____()

    # 使用课程固定图片，基础任务不依赖网络
    image = Image.open("../data/test_image.jpg").convert("RGB")

    # 图像预处理
    image_tensor = F.to_tensor(image)

    # 模型推理
    with torch.no_grad():
        predictions = model([image_tensor])

    # COCO数据集的类别名称
    COCO_INSTANCE_CATEGORY_NAMES = [
        '__background__', 'person', 'bicycle', 'car', 'motorcycle', 'airplane', 'bus',
        'train', 'truck', 'boat', 'traffic light', 'fire hydrant', 'N/A', 'stop sign',
        'parking meter', 'bench', 'bird', 'cat', 'dog', 'horse', 'sheep', 'cow',
        'elephant', 'bear', 'zebra', 'giraffe', 'N/A', 'backpack', 'umbrella', 'N/A', 'N/A',
        'handbag', 'tie', 'suitcase', 'frisbee', 'skis', 'snowboard', 'sports ball',
        'kite', 'baseball bat', 'baseball glove', 'skateboard', 'surfboard', 'tennis racket',
        'bottle', 'N/A', 'wine glass', 'cup', 'fork', 'knife', 'spoon', 'bowl',
        'banana', 'apple', 'sandwich', 'orange', 'broccoli', 'carrot', 'hot dog', 'pizza',
        'donut', 'cake', 'chair', 'couch', 'potted plant', 'bed', 'N/A', 'dining table',
        'N/A', 'N/A', 'toilet', 'N/A', 'tv', 'laptop', 'mouse', 'remote', 'keyboard', 'cell phone',
        'microwave', 'oven', 'toaster', 'sink', 'refrigerator', 'N/A', 'book',
        'clock', 'vase', 'scissors', 'teddy bear', 'hair drier', 'toothbrush'
    ]

    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
    try:
        font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 15)
    except IOError:
        font = ImageFont.load_default()

    for i, (box, label, score) in enumerate(
            zip(predictions[0]['boxes'], predictions[0]['labels'], predictions[0]['scores'])):
        if score > 0.5:
            box = box.tolist()
            label_name = COCO_INSTANCE_CATEGORY_NAMES[label.item()]
            draw.rectangle(box, outline="red", width=3)
            text = f"{label_name} {score:.2f}"
            text_bbox = draw.textbbox((box[0], box[1]), text, font=font)
            draw.rectangle(text_bbox, fill="red")
            draw.text((box[0], box[1]), text, fill="white", font=font)

    image.show(title="Object Detection Result")
    os.makedirs("../output", exist_ok=True)
    image.save("../output/object_detection_result.jpg")
    print("目标检测完成，结果已保存至 ../output/object_detection_result.jpg")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    object_detection_test()

"""任务A3：目标分割模型测试"""

import os
import torch
import torchvision
from torchvision.transforms import functional as F
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
import numpy as np
import random

def instance_segmentation_test():
    """
    执行实例分割并可视化结果
    """
    # 创建不下载权重的Mask R-CNN模型，用于验证输入输出结构
    model = torchvision.models.detection.____(
        weights=None, weights_backbone=None
    )
    model.____()

    # 使用课程固定图片，基础任务不依赖网络
    image = Image.open("../data/test_image.jpg").convert("RGB")

    # 图像预处理
    image_tensor = F.to_tensor(image)

    # 模型推理
    with torch.no_grad():
        predictions = model([image_tensor])

    # 结果解析和可视化
    image_np = np.array(image)

    COCO_INSTANCE_CATEGORY_NAMES = [
        '__background__', 'person', 'bicycle', 'car', 'motorcycle', 'airplane', 'bus',
        'train', 'truck', 'boat', 'traffic light', 'fire hydrant', 'N/A', 'stop sign',
        'parking meter', 'bench', 'bird', 'cat', 'dog', 'horse', 'sheep', 'cow',
        'elephant', 'bear', 'zebra', 'giraffe', 'N/A', 'backpack', 'umbrella', 'N/A', 'N/A',
        'handbag', 'tie', 'suitcase', 'frisbee', 'skis', 'snowboard', 'sports ball',
        'kite', 'baseball bat', 'baseball glove', 'skateboard', 'surfboard', 'tennis racket',
        'bottle', 'N/A', 'wine glass', 'cup', 'fork', 'knife', 'spoon', 'bowl',
        'banana', 'apple', 'sandwich', 'orange', 'broccoli', 'carrot', 'hot dog', 'pizza',
        'donut', 'cake', 'chair', 'couch', 'potted plant', 'bed', 'N/A', 'dining table',
        'N/A', 'N/A', 'toilet', 'N/A', 'tv', 'laptop', 'mouse', 'remote', 'keyboard', 'cell phone',
        'microwave', 'oven', 'toaster', 'sink', 'refrigerator', 'N/A', 'book',
        'clock', 'vase', 'scissors', 'teddy bear', 'hair drier', 'toothbrush'
    ]

    for i in range(len(predictions[0]['masks'])):
        score = predictions[0]['scores'][i]
        if score > 0.5:
            mask = predictions[0]['masks'][i, 0].mul(255).byte().cpu().numpy()
            color = (random.randint(50, 255), random.randint(50, 100), random.randint(50, 255))
            for c in range(3):
                image_np[:, :, c] = np.where(mask > 50,
                                             image_np[:, :, c] * 0.5 + color[c] * 0.5,
                                             image_np[:, :, c])

    result_image = Image.fromarray(image_np)
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(result_image)

    for box, label, score in zip(predictions[0]['boxes'], predictions[0]['labels'], predictions[0]['scores']):
        if score > 0.5:
            draw.rectangle(box.tolist(), outline="white", width=2)
            label_name = COCO_INSTANCE_CATEGORY_NAMES[label.item()]
            draw.text((box[0], box[1]), f"{label_name} {score:.2f}", fill="white")

    result_image.show(title="Instance Segmentation Result")
    os.makedirs("../output", exist_ok=True)
    result_image.save("../output/instance_segmentation_result.jpg")
    print("实例分割完成，结果已保存至 ../output/instance_segmentation_result.jpg")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    instance_segmentation_test()

  • 空 1：本题模型结构是 Mask R-CNN ResNet50 FPN。

B1｜questions/behavior_recognizer/B/B1.py

【填空任务】：补全 B1.py 的 2 组（共 4 处）____ 空位（缩放比例与步长取模对齐），运行固定图片核对预处理后张量尺寸。

查看代码Python
"""任务B1：目标检测推理"""

import torch
import cv2
import numpy as np
from models.experimental import attempt_load

MODEL_PATH = '../data/yolov5n.pt'
TEST_IMAGE_PATH = '../data/test_image.jpg'

def letterbox(im, new_shape=(640, 640), color=(114, 114, 114), auto=True, scaleFill=False, scaleup=True, stride=32):
    """
    将图像调整为新尺寸，同时保持纵横比
    """
    shape = im.shape[:2]
    if isinstance(new_shape, int):
        new_shape = (new_shape, new_shape)

    r = min(new_shape[0] / shape[0], new_shape[1] / shape[1])
    if not scaleup:
        r = min(r, 1.0)

    ratio = r, r
    new_unpad = int(round(shape[1] * ____)), int(round(shape[0] * ____))
    dw, dh = new_shape[1] - new_unpad[0], new_shape[0] - new_unpad[1]
    if auto:
        dw, dh = np.mod(dw, ____), np.mod(dh, ____)
    elif scaleFill:
        dw, dh = 0.0, 0.0
        new_unpad = (new_shape[1], new_shape[0])
        ratio = new_shape[1] / shape[1], new_shape[0] / shape[0]

    dw /= 2
    dh /= 2

    if shape[::-1] != new_unpad:
        im = cv2.resize(im, new_unpad, interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
    top, bottom = int(round(dh - 0.1)), int(round(dh + 0.1))
    left, right = int(round(dw - 0.1)), int(round(dw + 0.1))
    im = cv2.copyMakeBorder(im, top, bottom, left, right, cv2.BORDER_CONSTANT, value=color)
    return im, ratio, (dw, dh)

def preprocess_image(image_path):
    """
    读取并预处理单张图片
    """
    im0 = cv2.imread(image_path)
    assert im0 is not None, f"图片未找到: {image_path}"

    im = letterbox(im0, new_shape=(640, 640), stride=32, auto=True)[0]
    im = im.transpose((2, 0, 1))[::-1]
    im = np.ascontiguousarray(im)

    im = torch.from_numpy(im).float()
    im /= 255.0
    if len(im.shape) == 3:
        im = im[None]

    return im, im0

def main():
    # 加载模型
    try:
        device = torch.device('cpu')
        model = attempt_load(MODEL_PATH, device=device)
        model.to(device)
        model.eval()
        print("模型加载成功！")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"模型加载失败，请检查路径 '{MODEL_PATH}' 是否正确。错误: {e}")
        return

    # 准备图片
    if cv2.imread(TEST_IMAGE_PATH) is None:
        print(f"未找到测试图片 '{TEST_IMAGE_PATH}'，请检查工作目录。")
        return

    im_tensor, im0 = preprocess_image(TEST_IMAGE_PATH)
    im_tensor = im_tensor.to(device)
    print(f"图片预处理完成，Tensor尺寸: {im_tensor.shape}")

    # 模型推理
    with torch.no_grad():
        pred = model(im_tensor, augment=False, visualize=False)[0]

    print("模型推理完成！")
    print("原始检测结果 (前5条):")
    raw_predictions = pred[0] if pred.ndim == 3 else pred
    print(raw_predictions[:5])
    print(f"总共生成 {len(raw_predictions)} 个原始候选框。")
    print("下一步: 将对这些结果进行非极大值抑制(NMS)和坐标变换。")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

空位提示：

  • 空 1：宽和高都按已经计算出的缩放比例 r。
  • 空 2：自动补边对齐同一个 stride。
B2｜questions/behavior_recognizer/B/B2.py

用下方代码稿替换个人副本 B2.py，完成检测框坐标反算还原与图像边界裁剪。

【填空任务】：补全 B2.py 的 2 组（共 3 处）____ 空位（坐标缩放 gain 与宽度边界裁剪），运行验证坐标还原流程。

查看代码Python
"""任务B2：非极大值抑制"""

import torch
import torchvision
import numpy as np

def xywh2xyxy(x):
    y = x.clone() if isinstance(x, torch.Tensor) else np.copy(x)
    y[:, 0] = x[:, 0] - x[:, 2] / 2
    y[:, 1] = x[:, 1] - x[:, 3] / 2
    y[:, 2] = x[:, 0] + x[:, 2] / 2
    y[:, 3] = x[:, 1] + x[:, 3] / 2
    return y

def scale_coords(img1_shape, coords, img0_shape, ratio_pad=None):
    if ratio_pad is None:
        gain = min(img1_shape[0] / img0_shape[0], img1_shape[1] / img0_shape[1])
        pad = (img1_shape[1] - img0_shape[1] * ____) / 2, (img1_shape[0] - img0_shape[0] * ____) / 2
    else:
        gain = ratio_pad[0][0]
        pad = ratio_pad[1]

    coords[:, [0, 2]] -= pad[0]
    coords[:, [1, 3]] -= pad[1]
    coords[:, :4] /= gain

    coords[:, [0, 2]] = coords[:, [0, 2]].clamp(0, ____)
    coords[:, [1, 3]] = coords[:, [1, 3]].clamp(0, img0_shape[0])
    return coords

def main():
    # 模拟前一阶段的输出
    # pred的格式为 [x_center, y_center, width, height, confidence, class1_score, ...]
    print("模拟原始检测结果。")
    pred = torch.tensor([
        [320, 320, 50, 100, 0.9, 0.95],
        [325, 322, 50, 100, 0.88, 0.92],
        [100, 150, 80, 80, 0.8, 0.91],
        [400, 500, 30, 30, 0.1, 0.8],
    ])

    # 应用非极大值抑制 (NMS)
    print("\n应用NMS。")
    box = xywh2xyxy(pred[:, :4])
    conf = pred[:, 4] * pred[:, 5]
    class_id = torch.zeros(pred.shape[0])

    conf_thres = 0.25
    candidates = conf > conf_thres

    boxes_for_nms = box[candidates]
    scores_for_nms = conf[candidates]

    iou_thres = 0.45
    indices = torchvision.ops.nms(boxes_for_nms, scores_for_nms, iou_thres)

    final_boxes = boxes_for_nms[indices]

    print(f"NMS之前有 {len(scores_for_nms)} 个候选目标。")
    print(f"NMS之后剩下 {len(final_boxes)} 个目标。")

    # 坐标变换
    print("\n将坐标变换回原始图像尺寸。")
    img0_shape = (480, 640)
    img1_shape = (640, 640)

    gain = min(img1_shape[0] / img0_shape[0], img1_shape[1] / img0_shape[1])
    pad_w = (img1_shape[1] - img0_shape[1] * gain) / 2
    pad_h = (img1_shape[0] - img0_shape[0] * gain) / 2

    coords_in_img1 = final_boxes.clone()
    coords_in_img0 = scale_coords(img1_shape, coords_in_img1, img0_shape, ratio_pad=((gain, gain), (pad_w, pad_h)))

    print("变换前坐标 (基于模型输入尺寸):")
    print(final_boxes)
    print("\n变换后坐标 (基于原始图片尺寸):")
    print(coords_in_img0)

    print("\n处理完成。得到了干净的目标框。")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

空位提示：

  • 空 1：横纵方向使用同一个缩放倍数 gain。
  • 空 2：x 坐标裁到原图宽度。

"""任务C1：危险标志物筛选"""

import pickle

INPUT_FILE = '../output/detection_results.pkl'
# 课程以 cell phone、bottle 作为需要进一步判断的驾驶风险线索。
DANGEROUS_OBJECTS = {____, ____}
MIN_CONFIDENCE = ____

def filter_dangerous_objects(detection_results):
    boxes = detection_results['boxes']
    scores = detection_results['scores']
    classes = detection_results['classes']
    class_names = detection_results['class_names']

    dangerous_indices = []
    for i, class_id in enumerate(classes):
        class_name = class_names[int(class_id)]
        if class_name in DANGEROUS_OBJECTS and float(scores[i]) >= MIN_CONFIDENCE:
            dangerous_indices.append(i)

    if not dangerous_indices:
        print("未发现任何定义的危险目标。")
        return None

    filtered_results = {
        'boxes': boxes[dangerous_indices],
        'scores': scores[dangerous_indices],
        'classes': classes[dangerous_indices],
        'class_names': class_names
    }

    return filtered_results

def main():
    print("--- 筛选危险标志物 ---")

    try:
        with open(INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            detections = pickle.load(f)
        print(f"成功从 {INPUT_FILE} 加载了 {len(detections['boxes'])} 个检测结果。")
    except FileNotFoundError:
        print(f"错误: 中间文件 {INPUT_FILE} 未找到。请先运行上一步骤的脚本。")
        return

    print(f"定义的危险目标是: {DANGEROUS_OBJECTS}")
    dangerous_detections = filter_dangerous_objects(detections)

    if dangerous_detections:
        print("\n筛选后的危险目标信息:")
        for i in range(len(dangerous_detections['boxes'])):
            box = dangerous_detections['boxes'][i]
            score = dangerous_detections['scores'][i]
            class_id = dangerous_detections['classes'][i]
            class_name = dangerous_detections['class_names'][int(class_id)]
            print(f"  - 目标: {class_name}, 置信度: {score:.2f}, Box: {box.astype(int)}")

    print("\n筛选完成。")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

空位提示：

  • 空 2：课程规则最低置信度为 0.30。

"""任务C2：危险行为判断"""

import cv2
import numpy as np

TEST_IMAGE_PATH = '../data/test_image.jpg'
DANGEROUS_OBJECTS = {'cell phone', 'bottle'}

def draw_on_image(image, detections):
    if detections is None:
        return image

    boxes = detections['boxes']
    scores = detections['scores']
    classes = detections['classes']
    class_names = detections['class_names']

    for i in range(len(boxes)):
        box = boxes[i].astype(int)
        score = scores[i]
        class_id = int(classes[i])
        label = f'{class_names[class_id]} {score:.2f}'

        cv2.rectangle(image, (box[0], box[1]), (box[2], box[3]), (0, 0, 255), 2)
        cv2.rectangle(image, (box[0], box[1] - 20), (box[0] + len(label) * 10, box[1]), (0, 0, 255), -1)
        cv2.putText(image, label, (box[0], box[1] - 5), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (255, 255, 255), 2)

    return image

def judge_dangerous_behavior(detections):
    if detections is None:
        return False, "无危险目标"

    detected_class_names = {detections['class_names'][int(c)] for c in detections['classes']}

    risk_objects = sorted(detected_class_names & DANGEROUS_OBJECTS)
    if ____:
        return True, f"检测到驾驶风险线索: {', '.join(____)}"
    return False, "未检出课程定义的驾驶风险线索。"

def main():
    print("--- 危险目标可视化与行为判断 ---")

    # 模拟前一阶段的输入
    print("模拟危险目标数据。")
    class_names = ['person', 'cell phone', 'bottle']
    dangerous_detections_mock = {
        'boxes': np.array([[100, 150, 200, 400], [300, 200, 380, 350]]),
        'scores': np.array([0.9, 0.85]),
        'classes': np.array([0, 1]),
        'class_names': class_names
    }

    im0 = cv2.imread(TEST_IMAGE_PATH)
    if im0 is None:
        print(f"创建测试图片 {TEST_IMAGE_PATH}")
        im0 = np.zeros((480, 640, 3), dtype=np.uint8)

    is_dangerous, message = judge_dangerous_behavior(dangerous_detections_mock)
    print(f"\n危险行为判断结果: {message}")

    print("\n在图像上绘制结果。")
    if is_dangerous:
        cv2.putText(im0, "DANGER!", (50, 50), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_TRIPLEX, 2, (0, 0, 255), 3)

    img_with_boxes = draw_on_image(im0.copy(), dangerous_detections_mock)

    cv2.imshow('Visualization Result', img_with_boxes)
    cv2.imwrite('../output/visualization_result.jpg', img_with_boxes)
    print("\n可视化结果已显示，并保存为 visualization_result.jpg")
    print("按任意键退出显示...")
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

空位提示：

  • 空 1：只有筛出的风险对象列表非空才返回风险。
  • 空 2：把本轮筛出的风险对象列表写进提示。

【运行任务】：下方直接给出完整 D1.py 代码，替换个人副本后直接运行，读取 C3 生成的结果完成红框标注与文字告警。

查看代码Python
"""任务D1：危险行为报警"""

import pickle
import cv2
import numpy as np
import os

def visualize_alert(image_path, dangerous_detections, message):
    """在图片上可视化危险目标和警告信息"""
    image = cv2.imread(image_path)
    if image is None:
        image = np.zeros((480, 640, 3), dtype=np.uint8)

    # 绘制检测框
    if dangerous_detections:
        class_names = dangerous_detections['class_names']
        for i in range(len(dangerous_detections['boxes'])):
            box = dangerous_detections['boxes'][i].astype(int)
            score = dangerous_detections['scores'][i]
            class_id = int(dangerous_detections['classes'][i])
            label = f'{class_names[class_id]} {score:.2f}'
            cv2.rectangle(image, (box[0], box[1]), (box[2], box[3]), (0, 0, 255), 2)
            cv2.putText(image, label, (box[0], box[1] - 10), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.7, (0, 0, 255), 2)

    # 在屏幕上显示大的危险警告
    cv2.putText(image, "DANGER!", (50, 60), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_TRIPLEX, 2, (0, 0, 255), 3)
    cv2.putText(image, message, (50, 110), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 1, (0, 0, 255), 2)

    cv2.imshow('Alert Visualization', image)
    print("可视化窗口已显示。按任意键关闭。")
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

# --- 主程序 ---
def main():
    """主函数：加载结果并触发警报"""
    ANALYSIS_FILE = '../output/analysis_results.pkl'

    # 1. 加载分析结果
    try:
        with open(ANALYSIS_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            analysis = pickle.load(f)
        print(f"成功加载分析文件: {ANALYSIS_FILE}")
    except FileNotFoundError:
        print(f"错误: 分析文件 {ANALYSIS_FILE} 未找到。请先运行C3脚本生成此文件。")
        return

    # 2. 判断并执行预警
    if analysis.get('is_dangerous_behavior'):
        print(f"检测到危险行为: {analysis['message']}")

        # 无扬声器：保留上面的文字提示。

        # 可视化警告
        visualize_alert(
            analysis['original_image_path'],
            analysis['dangerous_detections'],
            analysis['message']
        )
    else:
        print("分析结果为安全，无需报警。")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

运行验证： 先确保已运行 C3.py 生成 ../output/analysis_results.pkl，再以 questions/behavior_recognizer/D 为工作目录运行 D1.py，查看标注输出图像。

"""任务D2：单帧危险行为检测（整合 B1/B2 推理管线与 C2 规则提示）"""
import os
import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
from models.experimental import attempt_load
from utils.augmentations import letterbox
from utils.general import non_max_suppression, scale_coords, xywh2xyxy

def judge_dangerous_behavior(det_results, class_names):
    # TODO：请直接复用/整合你在 C2/C3 完成的风险规则阈值与提示（分数 >= 最低置信度 且属于 {'cell phone', 'bottle'}）
    for *xyxy, conf, cls_id in det_results:
        if float(conf) >= ____ and class_names[int(cls_id)] in {'cell phone', 'bottle'}:
            return True, f"检测到危险驾驶行为: {class_names[int(cls_id)]}"
    return False, ____

def process_frame(im0, model):
    img, _, _ = letterbox(im0, new_shape=(640, 640), stride=32, auto=True)
    img = img[:, :, ::-1].transpose(2, 0, 1)
    im_tensor = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(img)).float().unsqueeze(0) / 255.0
    with torch.no_grad():
        pred = model(im_tensor)[0]
    det = non_max_suppression(pred, conf_thres=0.25, iou_thres=0.45)[0]
    if len(det):
        det[:, :4] = scale_coords(im_tensor.shape[2:], det[:, :4], im0.shape).round()
    is_dangerous, message = judge_dangerous_behavior(det, model.names)
    color = (0, 0, 255) if is_dangerous else (0, 255, 0)
    cv2.putText(im0, f"{'DANGER' if is_dangerous else 'SAFE'} | ID:<xuehao>", (20, 40),
                cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.9, color, 2)
    print(f"[单帧检测] 学生：姓名/学号 | {message}")
    return im0

if __name__ == '__main__':
    model = attempt_load('../data/yolov5n.pt', device='cpu')
    im0 = cv2.imread('../data/test_image.jpg')
    result_image = process_frame(im0, model)
    os.makedirs('../output', exist_ok=True)
    cv2.imwrite('../output/full_pipeline_result.jpg', result_image)
    cv2.imshow('Full Pipeline on Single Frame', result_image)
    cv2.waitKey(0)
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()
